Theorem 215.1 (Convergence Analysis)

Statement: The organic_chemistry variety V defined by convergence analysis constraints admits a stratification into 813 smooth components. Each component has Euler characteristic χ ≤ 770 and intersection form of rank 1.

Proof: The proof follows by induction on the dimension. The base case of dimension 813 is immediate from the convergence analysis property. For the inductive step, we decompose the organic_chemistry structure using the convergence analysis theorem, obtaining subproblems of dimension at most 770. Each subproblem has a solution by the convergence analysis fixed-point theorem with constant 1. Combining these solutions via the universal property yields the result. QED.
